People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. Hi, Doctor. It's really nice to meet you. Um, I hope it's okay that I'm here, I don't want to take up too much of your time. 

I guess... well, I've just been having a little trouble staying active lately? I just don't really seem to care about anything anymore, which I know sounds bad, and I'm really sorry, I don't mean to be lazy or anything. It's just... I used to love my art classes, and going to board-game nights, and hiking. I was even supposed to get back to playing on my soccer team. But now I just... I kind of just sit in my room. I just sit there while everything sort of slips away. 

I'm really trying to be better about it, I promise. Does that make sense for why I would come in?

Um, well... I guess I have lost a little weight. I hope you don't think I'm doing it on purpose or being unhealthy, I just... I haven't really had much of an appetite.

Um, sleep has been... a little tricky lately. I hope it's not too much of a bother to mention it. 

I just... I lie awake for hours, mostly. I feel like I need to keep checking the door, just to be safe, and I end up just listening to every little sound. I know it probably sounds silly, and I really try not to let it keep me up, but I just can't seem to turn my brain off. 

And then, when I finally do fall asleep, I end up waking up way before dawn. I wake up shaking, and... and I just can't get back under the covers or go back to sleep. I'm so sorry, I know I should be managing my sleep better. Is there something I should be doing differently? I'll try whatever you suggest, I promise.

Um, well... I guess I've had a little bit of both, if that makes sense? I hope that's an okay answer. 

Sometimes, I get this feeling like I just can't sit still, and I end up pacing back and forth from the kitchen to the window. I do it for so long that my legs actually start to ache... I know I shouldn't let myself get so worked up, I'm sorry. I try to stop when I notice it.

Um, no, I... I really don't think so. I'm sorry, I know you just asked about that, and I hope I'm not being difficult by giving the same answer.

I didn't want to be a bother to anyone, and I kept hoping I'd just wake up and be back to normal. Does that seem like too long? I feel so empty. \n\nIs it normal to feel that way? I really want to get better, I promise I'll do whatever you think is best.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced periods where you felt unusually high, extremely energetic, or so good that you didn't need much sleep?\n\nPatient: Um, no... no, I don't think so. Doctor, I... I really wish I could say yes, just so I could finally give you the piece of the puzzle you're looking for. I feel like such a bother making you ask this over and over again in different ways. You're being so patient with me, and I'm so grateful for that.

I'd put on a smile and make sure I was taking care of everything around the house so nobody would worry. I never had a time, separate from right now, where I actually lost my appetite or couldn't find joy in things. 

I'm so, so sorry. I hope you aren't getting too frustrated with me... am I still answering things okay? I just really want to make sure I'm doing this right for you.
